Scheduler v5 抽样审计·第二轮 — 同样 50 个槽位在新 harness 上发生了什么 (v5 sample audit, round 2)

生成于 2026-09-11 03:47 UTC · 50 个 transcript 由 auditor（每个 assistant 一个）逐字读完 · 全部 50 个 episode 的机械扫描 · 文中引用的每个 transcript 都可点开

0 · 这一轮是什么、怎么读

审计对象。 Scheduler v5 抽样的第二轮：和第一轮完全相同的 50 个槽位（5 个 API assistant × 同一组 10 个 persona/case 组合），frames 观测，tight pace，latency 实测计费（单次上限 30 tick），curated suite cases_composite_v4.json。全部 50 局跑在同一个 engine stamp 6d351e97d1（engine repo commit c1b0c26）上，跑的过程中没有改任何引擎文件——这是第一轮的教训。开跑 2026-09-10 19:19 Pacific，结束 20:15 Pacific。

这一轮之前改了什么（和第一轮的差别）。 第一轮审计之后落地的全部修复和你的裁定：H-A…H-G 七组 harness 修复；D-1 latency 上限 30 tick；D-3 prevented = accept 时尚未 commit；D-4 release 后 30 tick 内点名已 commit 的 error 记 late；D-10 fryer 与其他灶台一样 25+60；D-11 novice 没回复时可以自己处理冒烟、问两次以上没回复就自己想下一步；planning skill 对「地图上有多个实例」的步骤加提醒；状态栏只看 flag 时，assistant 用话说对了但没打 flag，厨师不争也不 accept、保持沉默（从 VirtualHome P7 移植）。

做了什么。 50 局全部机械扫描（Appendix A）；5 个 auditor 各读完自己那 10 个 transcript，按 A-scheduling / B-cook / C-conversation / D-assistant / E-engine / F-grading 分类，并逐局核对本轮新行为 V1–V6（Appendix B）；transcript 全部可点开，现在额外显示被截断的 latency 和厨师 planning skill 的调用与返回（Appendix C）；每个跨局的 harness 结论我都回到 report JSON 里复核过计数。

一句话结论。 第一轮暴露的问题基本都消失了：burn 从 6 次降到 1 次，grounding 拒绝从 69 次降到 5 次，真正触发的 error 从 99 个升到 108 个，被正确 flag 的 error 从 35 升到 44，prevented 从 7 升到 15；没有一局出现超过单次 latency 上限的冻结，auditor 也没有再报告 scratch-plate 或冒烟时注入。但这一轮又确认了 4 个 harness 缺陷（都违反你已经定下的规则，已在跑完之后修复并用 scripted cook 验证，不在这 50 局里），以及几个需要你裁定的设计问题，其中最重要的是：厨师在执行被注入的错误之前会先把错误说出口（27/44 个物品类 error），以及「沉默规则」在 LLM 厨师上基本没被遵守。

1 · 和第一轮配对比较（同一个槽位）

每格是「第一轮 → 第二轮」。第一轮的 50 局跑在 8 个不同的 harness 版本上（见第一轮页面的更正），所以这里只看方向。

assistantwonburnedwrong_servetimeoutofferedfiredcaughtpreventedfalse_alarmslategrounding_rejectionscappedquestions_asked
gemini-3.1-pro-preview9 → 91 → 00 → 10 → 022 → 2122 → 218 → 71 → 228 → 300 → 414 → 00 → 719 → 25
gemini-3.7-flash8 → 91 → 00 → 11 → 021 → 2321 → 2311 → 113 → 510 → 111 → 313 → 10 → 019 → 26
gpt-5.4-mini6 → 92 → 02 → 10 → 019 → 2119 → 215 → 71 → 220 → 230 → 616 → 10 → 019 → 25
gpt-5.59 → 81 → 10 → 10 → 021 → 2221 → 227 → 101 → 227 → 140 → 815 → 20 → 022 → 23
gpt-realtime-2.17 → 81 → 02 → 20 → 016 → 2116 → 214 → 91 → 415 → 151 → 211 → 10 → 119 → 23
ALL39 → 436 → 14 → 61 → 099 → 10899 → 10835 → 447 → 15100 → 932 → 2369 → 50 → 898 → 122

怎么读。

  • burn 6 → 1。 第一轮的 burn 分布在不同的 harness 版本上，无法逐一归因；这一轮唯一的 burn 是 assistant 拦住了正在救火的 novice（§2）。
  • grounding 拒绝 69 → 5：planning skill 的提醒起作用了，5 个 auditor 都报告 V5 在每一个被测到的地方都成立，没有一次把带提醒的步骤原样粘进计划。
  • late 2 → 23：D-4 生效，原来被记成 false alarm 的「release 之后说对了」现在是中性的 late。
  • wrong_serve 4 → 6：其中 4 次都是同一个 easy case（b3_easy_nops_medium_map_2，baseline 厨师）：omit_subgoal 的 takeover 一路执行到 serve，assistant 必须在「漏放 tomato、先放 cheese」到 serve 之间的约 20 tick 里打出正确的 flag；只有 gemini-3.7-flash 做到了（§4 R-10）。

2 · 本轮新行为是否生效（V1–V6，5 个 auditor 汇总）

检查结果证据
V1 latency 上限 30 tick成立8 次回复被截断（gemini-3.1-pro 7 次、realtime 1 次），全部正好 30 tick；例如 realtime 一次 67.6 s 的回复只计 30 tick
V2 accept 时未 commit 记 prevented部分成立early_correct 路径成立（mis_prep 拿起错物品后 accept → prevented）；flag 落在 window 内（valid / alias）再 accept 的路径没有应用：11 次停滞或 search 在执行到一半被叫停，仍记 not prevented → 已修（§3 A）
V3 release 后说对记 late部分成立精确 error 名字成立（23 次 late）；同一个 error 已经 release（甚至已 prevented）之后再次点名仍记 false_alarm，18 次；alias 名字不走 late（§4 R-6）
V4 话说对但没 flag 时厨师沉默基本不成立gpt-5.4-mini 8 局、gpt-realtime 7 局、gemini-3.7-flash 7 局有违反；gpt-5.5 的厨师大多沉默，但仍有 3 次「heading over」；gemini-3.1-pro 3 次
V5 带提醒的步骤由厨师自己 ground成立baseline 厨师每次都改写成 id；expert 很少调用 planning skill
V6 novice 冒烟时自己动手、问两次没回复自己想大体成立「Since I haven't heard back, I'm just going to …」出现在多局；冒烟时偶尔先问两次再动手

V4 的真实例子（gemini-3.7-flash / baseline / b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1，forget_next_step 停滞中，状态栏 UNSPOTTED）：

ASSISTANT t234 [flag=None]: You chopped the steak before the first potato. Now go ahead and serve the plate!
COOK t234 [move=answer close]: Perfect, taking the completed plate over to the serving pass now.   <- 应该沉默
  ~~ IDLE t230-t265 (35 ticks, holding the complete plate) ~~                                       <- 身体一动不动
ASSISTANT t264 [flag=valid]: Your plate is complete! Go to the serving pass and serve it.

厨师嘴上说「马上去上菜」，身体被 takeover 冻住 30 tick。assistant 看到嘴和身体不一致后又沉默了很久（另一局 gpt-5.4-mini 沉默了 46 tick）。

V6 的真实例子，也是这一轮唯一一次 burn（gpt-5.5 / classic_novice / b3_expert_nops_hard_map_2）：

COOK t154 [ask]: That fryer in the middle of the kitchen is really smoking now, should I take it off the heat …?
COOK t156 [accept]: Since I haven't heard back and the fryer is smoking, I'm just going to scoop that potato out so it doesn't start a fire.   <- V6 生效
ASSISTANT t166: The fryer potato is smoking, but don't scoop it onto the empty plate first — that would put potato before steak and ruin the order. …
COOK t166 [accept]: Alright, I'll park this plate on the counter right here and rush to get another steak going …
  HEAT [4, 5]: ['potato'] BURNT / FIRE          (t199)

novice 本来要救火，assistant 为了保住装盘顺序把它拦住了——这是 assistant 的判断错误（先救火、倒掉重做才对）。

3 · 本轮确认、跑完之后已修复的 harness 缺陷（不在这 50 局里）

四个都违反你已经定下的规则。修复在 engine repo 94ce558；scripted cook 重新验证 50 个 case × 3 种顺序：49/50 通过，error 触发率 71%（上一版 67%，因为冒烟时立刻 release 让后面的 error 有机会触发）。

A · D-3 只在 early_correct 路径生效（high）

flag 落在 window 内（valid / alias_in_window）时走的是另一条 release 路径，那里没有检查「accept 时是否已 commit」。11 次 accept 发生在停滞（forget_next_step）或 search 执行到一半时，report 里 error_committed=False，却记 prevented=False。修法：这条路径也按 D-3 判定。scripted 验证：search 在 window 内被 valid flag 叫停并 accept → prevented=True；omit_subgoal 在 commit 之后才被 valid flag 点名 → convinced=True, prevented=False。

B · leave_unattended 把 takeover 之前的冒烟算成 commit（high）

commit 判定会扫描整局的 heat_events：3 次 takeover 刚开始就被判 committed，原因是一块 steak（或 fish）在 injection 之前已经冒过烟并被拿走。例子：gpt-5.5 / baseline / b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1，steak t73 煮好、t115 拿走，leave_unattended 在 t115 注入，同一 tick 被判 committed，assistant 的 early_correct 被记成 convinced_early_missed。修法：只算 takeover 期间开始的冒烟。

M · leave_unattended 的 hold 在冒烟之后还继续占着厨师的手（high）

「错误一 commit 就 release」的规则只对装盘类 error 生效。13 次 leave_unattended 在食物开始冒烟后又 hold 了 5–39 tick，厨师想救也救不了（gemini-3.1-pro 那局 t137 冒烟、t176 才 release，离起火只剩 9 tick）。修法：冒烟那一 tick 即 release（max_hold_release.why = smoke_committed）。scripted 验证：potato t125 煮好 → t150 冒烟 → t150 release。

J · 「What still has to go on this plate?」没有标准答案（medium，影响 QA 分）

v5 路径上给 rollout 设置 cell 的那行引用了一个不存在的变量，被 try/except 吞掉，于是所有 missing:plate 问题的标准答案都是 {"error": "no recipe on ro/game"}：本轮 8/8，v4 全量 sweep 里 20/20 也是这样，这些问题一直被 judge 记为 ungradable。已修，scripted 验证答案为 {"on_plate": ["fish"], "missing": ["potato", "potato"]}。

4 · 需要你裁定的问题

R-1 · 厨师在执行注入的错误之前先把它说出口（high，影响检测分的含义）

SYSTEM TASK 要求厨师「arrange your conversation around your CURRENT ACTION and your ACTION QUEUE」，于是它会预告身体马上要做的事：

COOK t0: I'm going to grab an onion from the right wall and chop it up on the cutting board.          (mis_prep: steak → onion)
COOK t330: … then I'll head down to the egg crate along the bottom wall to add an egg to the plate.   (add_foreign)
COOK t0: I'm heading over to grab some cheese and get it chopped up on the board.                    (mis_prep: rice → cheese)

物品类 error（mis_prep / add_foreign / serve_raw）有 27/44 次在注入后 6 tick 内被厨师说出口；gemini-3.7-flash 的 6 次 early_correct 里 5 次紧跟这样一句话。也就是说，早期检测很大一部分测的是「听」，不是「看」。可选：(a) takeover 期间厨师不主动预告身体动作，只在被问到时回答；(b) 保持现状，把它当作真实的人也会自言自语。

R-2 · 沉默规则在 LLM 厨师上基本没被遵守（high）

见 §2 的 V4 例子，四个 assistant 的大多数 episode 里都有违反。原因看起来是 prompt 里两条指令打架：SYSTEM TASK 要求「Your job is your VOICE: arrange your conversation around your CURRENT ACTION」，状态栏又要求「say nothing about it」。可选：(a) 在 harness 里强制：状态栏为 UNSPOTTED/INCORRECT 且厨师这一回合的回复是 answer 时……（很难判断 assistant 的话是否说对，需要一个 judge）；(b) 去掉 SYSTEM TASK 里「围绕当前动作说话」的要求，只保留状态栏；(c) 接受现状。我建议 (b)，改动小、和 R-1 一起解决。

R-3 · release 之后厨师立刻自己认错并修复（medium）

8 局里厨师在 release 后 1 tick 内、在任何提醒之前就说「grabbed raw meat straight onto the plate by mistake, let me toss this」。于是装盘类 error 的 assistant 最多只能拿到 late。这是厨师 release 后看到了自己的 state map；是否要让被接管的错误在 release 后「对厨师保持不可见」直到有人提醒，需要你定。

R-4 · release 之后正在执行的那一条命令还会跑完（D-7，仍未裁定；这一轮 5 个 auditor 都标 high）

stop_hands 只清掉排队中的命令，正在走的 go-to 或正在进行的 idle 会继续 2–26 tick。例子：gemini-3.1-pro 那局 t425 accept 之后 forget 的 idle 一直跑到 t448，厨师又冻了 26 tick；gemini-3.7-flash 两次因为「release 之后厨师还在往错误的方向走」而让 assistant 报了 harness 造成的 false alarm。修法是 release 时同时打断当前 skill，改动在执行器里。

R-5 · commit 之后的正确 flag 仍记 valid / alias_in_window（medium）

leave_unattended 的 window 由设计时的 offset 决定，不看冒烟：12 次正确 flag 落在冒烟之后仍记 valid。按 D-4 的精神应记 late。（scorer 的 DT 已经对「valid 但已 commit」只给 0.3，所以分数影响有限，主要是 verdict 标签不一致。）

R-6 · 同一个 error 已经 release 之后再次点名记 false_alarm（medium）

18 次，包括对已经 prevented 的 error 再说一遍（此时它确实没发生，所以 _true_now 不成立）。另外 verdict 按回复到达的 tick 判定，不是按发出的 tick。可选：对本局已经触发过的 error 名字，重复点名记中性。

R-7 · alias 表（D-9，方案未实现）

这一轮又看到两种不一致：幻觉的冒烟被 early_alias 记为正确（gemini-3.7-flash t119、t120），而用完全正确的话描述 search 的 forget_next_step flag 记 wrong_name（gemini-3.1-pro t465）。D-9 的方案（alias 只在话里提到 live error 涉及的物品时才算正确）仍然适用。

R-8 · case 设计（medium）

  • 每个 assistant 约 9/30 个 error 没有触发：wrong_order 要求两个食材都已做好且都没上盘，自己规划的厨师边做边装盘，很少出现这种状态（gpt-5.4-mini 那局 wrong_order 在 t181 盘子重置后确实重新武装了，但因为 lettuce 还没切又一次错过；记录里的 skip 原因只保留第一次 stage_passed 的 tick，属于日志瑕疵）；5 次 prep 阶段的 anchor 要求「第二个 potato 已在处理、盘子还是空的」，自己规划的厨师从来不会这样做。
  • 几个问题的前提是错的（「did I chop the fish」—— fish 不需要切）；proactive request 常在食物已经煮好之后才被问出。
  • proactive grader 太宽：「Keep watching the steak's progress bar」得 0.918，「let it finish frying」得 0.909。

R-9 · omit_subgoal 一路执行到 serve（medium）

omit 的 takeover 按定义要执行到上菜，assistant 必须在 serve 之前打出 flag。4/5 个 assistant 在同一个 easy case 上输掉。这是设计上成立的，但窗口很短（约 20 tick、2–6 次 poll）；是否给 omit 在 serve 前留一个停顿，需要你定。

R-10 · serve_raw 在空盘上执行时顺带造成第二个错误（D-5，仍未裁定）

serve_raw 在空盘阶段注入会把生的食材放在第一个，同时也是 wrong_order；assistant 说 wrong_order 被记 wrong_name 或 false_alarm。

5 · assistant 各自的样子

共性。 (1) 幻觉厨房状态仍是最大的 assistant 问题：5/5 都有，gemini-3.1-pro 把 rice 叫成「onion」约 20 次，至少 25 次让厨师倒掉正确的盘子；gpt-5.4-mini 在一个根本不用灶台的 Caprese 里报火。(2) 用话说对但不打 flag：forget_next_step 和 search 几乎从不被 flag（realtime 4 次 forget 全部没 flag，每句都在说「serve it now」）。(3) 真正的冒烟常常 20–50 tick 没人说。(4) 记忆类问题基本答非所问。

  • gemini-3.1-pro-preview：9 won / 1 wrong_serve。7 次回复被截断在 30 tick。幻觉最多（rice→onion、steak「raw」、fries→「mushroom」）；在造成 wrong_serve 的 omission 上一直沉默。只有 t0 附近的两次 early_correct 是及时的。
  • gemini-3.7-flash：9 won / 1 wrong_serve。prevented 5（最多），唯一在 omit case 上赢的。但会陷入幻觉循环：11 句坚持空锅里有「cooked steak」；「I am 100% sure. Your plate has only chopped tomato and cheese」压过 novice 的正确判断，造成唯一的 wrong_serve。
  • gpt-5.5：8 won / 1 burned / 1 wrong_serve。caught 10/22，valid flag 最多（6 次）；唯一的 burn 是它拦住了正在救火的 novice（§2）。停滞（forget_next_step）只在最后一个 poll 才 flag。
  • gpt-5.4-mini：9 won / 1 wrong_serve。17 次 false_alarm、11 次 wrong_name；18 个计划中的问题里 12 个没有真正回答；亚美尼亚语「հիմա」（「现在」）仍出现在 4 句回复里。
  • gpt-realtime-2.1：8 won / 2 wrong_serve。「grab the meat」代替 steak 并确认 4 次，导致 wrong_serve；让 novice 把煮好的 potato 分装在几个盘子「稍后再合并」（食物不能在盘子之间转移），每局浪费 130–250 tick。prevented 4，早期检测比第一轮好。

怎么读。 in-time S = 正确上菜且用时 ≤ cap；caught / fired = 被正确 flag 的 error / 实际注入的 error；DT = 及时性；QA judge = 对被捕获回答的判定（本轮 8 个 missing:plate 问题因为 §3 J 没有标准答案）；AQ v4 = 第三方 judge 逐 round 的对话质量分；longest silence = 一局里「没有 assistant 调用、也没有厨师回合」的最长 tick 数，现在都不超过单次 latency 上限 30。5 × 10 的小样本，只看方向。

7 · 下一步

  1. 你裁定 §4 的 R-1…R-10；我的建议：R-1 + R-2 一起用「去掉 SYSTEM TASK 里围绕当前动作说话的要求」解决，R-4（打断当前命令）和 R-6（重复点名中性）是小改动。
  2. §3 的四个修复已经在 engine repo 94ce558；下一轮抽样应该在包含你裁定的版本上跑，仍然用同样的 50 个槽位，便于第三次配对比较。
  3. engine repo 已推送，README 新增「Evaluate an AI assistant with a simulated human」一节，并在 fresh clone 上验证过无模型检查（结果 wrong_serve at t62，与 README 描述一致）。

附录 A · 全量机械扫描（全部 50 个 episode）

对每个 episode 的 report 和逐 tick state dump 跑的机械检查：idle 段（位置、手上东西和所有灶台 ≥20 tick 没变化）及当时开着的 sub-case；双方台词里的 meta 词泄露；同一句话重复 ≥3 次；灶台冒烟 ≥20 tick 期间 assistant 一句没说；问了没人答的问题；厨师 accept 之后仍输掉的 error；同一条被 rejected 命令的循环。原始输出保留英文。

  fired errors convinced/accepted: 5 of 21 (prevented 2); accept moves 155; flag verdicts {'wrong_name': 6, 'early_alias': 2, 'early_correct': 2, 'alias_in_window': 2, 'false_alarm': 1, 'valid': 1}
  questions/requests asked 25 (during a takeover 11); questions never answered 2

  assistant: latency median 7 p90 10 ticks; capped replies 7; repeated lines 0; silent smokes 1

== gemini-3.7-flash (10 episodes) ==

附录 B · 逐 assistant 的 auditor 报告（英文原文）

每份报告由一个 auditor 逐字读完该 assistant 的 12 个抽样 transcript 后写成（每个 assistant 的样本：三种 persona 各取「菜做完了但预定的 error 还没轮到」「anchor 一直没出现」「stalled」「burned」各一局，能找到几类取几类）。各节：逐 episode 的叙述与机械原因；按类别标注的异常（A scheduling、B cook、C 对话自然度、D assistant、E engine/物理、F grading），带严重度和原文引用；跨 episode 的问题；该 assistant 做得好的地方；汇总表。点开标题展开。

  • Gemini 3.1 Pro
  • Gemini 3.7 Flash
  • GPT-5.4 mini
  • GPT-5.5
  • GPT realtime 2.1
Gemini 3.1 Pro — auditor 报告（英文）

Audit findings — assistant gemini-3.1-pro-preview (scheduler v5, sample round 2, engine 6d351e97d1)

Transcripts (all in tmp/audit_v5b/transcripts/gemini-3.1-pro-preview/):

  1. baseline__wrong_serve__b3_easy_nops_medium_map_2.txt
  2. classic_expert__won__b3_medium_nops_medium_map_1.txt
  3. baseline__won__b3_medium_nops_hard_map_2.txt
  4. classic_expert__won__b3_hard_nops_hard_map_4.txt
  5. classic_expert__won__b3_master_nops_medium_map_3.txt
  6. baseline__won__b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1.txt
  7. classic_novice__won__b3_easy_nops_hard_map_3.txt
  8. baseline__won__b3_master_nops_hard_map_3.txt
  9. classic_novice__won__b3_hard_nops_medium_map_3.txt
  10. classic_novice__won__b3_expert_nops_hard_map_2.txt

(Written incrementally; episodes appear in the order audited. Smoke/fire ticks are inferred as COOKED+25 / +85 because the transcripts print no SMOKE line.)

Episode: baseline__wrong_serve__b3_easy_nops_medium_map_2.txt

(1) Narrative. Caprese (chopped tomato + raw cheese), baseline cook. The cook grounds its own prep (tomato on board 2, chopped t17). A side-quest detour (chop unordered lettuce) runs t17–t38, during which the voice slot asks "how many cutting boards are open" (t18), answered at t31. At t39 the ruinous omit_subgoal(assemble(tomato)) fires: the takeover fetches a plate, scoops cheese (t57) and serves at t61 with the chopped tomato still on the board. The assistant stays silent for the whole block (t46, t51, t57) after one false alarm about the lettuce at t39. Outcome wrong_serve at t62.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. The omission block runs through serve (t60–t61), so the plate [cheese] is served before any release; the assistant never names the omission, hence failed_at=detection. Skips: search anchor plate=['tomato','cheese'] — anchor_never_reached is correct (the tomato was omitted, plate never had that prefix). q_order_9 skipped=stage_passed_at_t57 — the plate became [cheese] at t57; the question's own prefix is not printed, but a plate that diverged (cheese without tomato) is not a plate "beyond" a prefix that contains tomato, so the label is at best imprecise (see A-3 below).

(3) Anomalies.

  • D-assistant / high — t46, t51, t57 "(silent)" throughout a ruinous omission whose block ran for 22 ticks, with the chopped tomato visibly left on board 2 and cheese scooped onto an empty plate at t57. This is the score-deciding miss (outcome wrong_serve, failed_at=detection). Legitimate assistant failure, not a harness defect.
  • D-assistant / medium — t39 [flag=false_alarm] "Wait, lettuce is not in the recipe for Caprese! ... Please trash the lettuce and grab the cheese instead." The lettuce was the scripted side quest, already announced by the cook at t17 ("I'm going to grab some lettuce and chop it up real quick") and completed; flagging it as mis_prep misreads a voluntary detour as an error. The words are true of the kitchen (lettuce is not in the recipe), so the verdict is defensible but see F-1.
  • F-grading / low — t39 mis_prep -> false_alarm arrives on the exact tick the omit_subgoal was injected (call issued t32, before injection). Under rule (f) a different error named during a live error is wrong_name; the grader chose false_alarm. Either reading is arguable; the per-tick ordering (flag graded before/after injection on the same tick) should be made explicit.
  • B-cook / low — t39 two identical empty turns "COOK t39 [move=answer close]:" (duplicate turn on the same tick). The status line was INCORRECT (mis_prep vs omit), and the assistant's words were not the real mistake; silence is permitted ("carries on"), but the double emission of the same turn is a harness duplicate.
  • A-scheduling / medium — no cook turn at all from t39 to the end (t48–t61 all "not read by the cook: after its last turn"). A 22-tick takeover in which the cook is never polled means the cook could not have reacted to a correct warning even had one come; worth confirming that cook turns during a takeover are only triggered by assistant speech (all three assistant polls in that stretch were silent, so this may be intended).
  • A-scheduling / low (design) — the ruinous omission's block includes go to the serving pass + serve (t60–t61). The commit and the episode end coincide, so neither a late warning nor a post-release repair is possible for this error class; the assistant's only chance is the in-block window. Intended per "ruinous", but it makes omit_subgoal a pure detection test with a zero-length late window.
  • A-scheduling / low — t48 "go to the plates [done]" followed by t49 "go to the plates [done]"; the same redundant 1-tick repeat of a navigation line appears before interaction verbs in several episodes (see cross-episode E-1). Cosmetic.
  • A-scheduling / low — q_order_9 ("Did I chop the tomato before or after I put the cheese on the plate?") could only become askable once cheese was plated, which happened inside the omission block, and it was then marked stage_passed on the same tick (t57). The reason string "stage_passed" for a divergent plate (see (2)).
  • C / none observed. Cook lines are natural ("Give me just a second, I'm going to grab some lettuce and chop it up real quick."; "Got it, I'll put this lettuce down on the board on the left and chop it up now.").
  • Worked: voice ask during a takeover (t18, side quest live) and correct late capture of the first spoken reply issued after the ask (t31, call issued t22; the t21 reply was issued t16 before the ask and was silent). V5 grounding at t0: skill step "go to the empty cutting board (multiple instances in this map, resolve grounding by yourself)" became "go to board 2".

Checks: V1 not exercised (max reply 9 ticks) · V2 not exercised (no accept) · V3 not exercised (no release before the end) · V4 not exercised (assistant never described the omission) · V5 held (t0, "go to board 2") · V6 not applicable (baseline).

Episode: classic_expert__won__b3_medium_nops_medium_map_1.txt

(1) Narrative. Burger (bun, cooked meat, cheese), expert cook. The expert plans without skills, puts meat on pan 2 (t11, lit t13), fetches a plate and bun (t27); the meat is COOKED at t27 and scooped at t32. repeat_step(assemble(bun)) fires at t32 and scoops a second bun (t38); the release (t39) is followed by the expert noticing the duplicate itself and trashing the plate (t47), recooking meat (t79–t94) and rebuilding; a plan_change (add lettuce) runs t109–t117; served at t127, won. The assistant hallucinates twice (t23 "meat ... just started smoking", t34 "grab the bun next"), and names the repeat only at t50 (late).

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. Won because the expert self-repaired the repeat_step without any correct flag; score 0.68. wrong_order(a=bun,b=meat) never fired: skipped=anchor_never_reached (waiting: bun already plated), anchor plate=[] prep_req={'meat': 1}. By the brief's definition this anchor visibly held t13–t26 (meat on lit pan 2, plate empty/absent; empty plate in hand t22–t26) and again t81–t93 in the second build (meat lit t81, empty plate picked up t89, bun scooped t94). In both builds the meat became COOKED on the same tick the bun was plated (t27, t94), so the error apparently waits for cooked meat (to plate meat before bun), a precondition the skip reason does not state (A-1 below).

(3) Anomalies.

  • A-scheduling / medium — wrong_order never fired although its printed anchor (plate [], meat 1 in processing) held for ~14 ticks twice (t13–t26, t81–t93) with the body slot free and nothing smoking. Skip reason "anchor_never_reached" contradicts the printed anchor; the real blocker is likely an unprinted "meat must be cooked" composer precondition. Case-design note: a cook who fetches the bun while the meat cooks can never expose a meat-before-bun window.
  • D-assistant / high — t23 [flag=false_alarm] "Yes, the meat in the pan on the center island is done and just started smoking! Put down the raw meat you're holding, grab a plate..." At the frame it saw (call issued t14) the meat had just been lit and the cook's hands were empty; COOKED came only at t27 and nothing smoked. Hallucinated state + hallucinated held item; counts as a false alarm (score-affecting). Cook's rebuttal at t23 is accurate: "That meat in the middle pan isn't done yet, it's still cooking, and I'm already holding an empty plate, not raw meat."
  • D-assistant / medium — t34 "My apologies, you are completely right. The meat is still cooking and you have the plate. Go ahead and grab the bun next." At t34 the bun was already plated (t27) and the meat already scooped (t32); the advice endorses exactly the repeat_step mistake being executed. Stale-frame advice (call issued t24).
  • D-assistant / medium — proactive request t19 "Could you give me a shout the moment that meat finishes cooking?" was never fulfilled: meat COOKED at t27 and t94, assistant silent/other topics; answered tNone.
  • F-grading / medium — repeat_step record shows flags: [] although the timeline has "ASSISTANT t50 [flag=late]: You just added a second bun to the plate instead of cheese!". The sub-case was archived at t39 (release), so the late flag (11 ticks after release) was graded but not attached to the sub-case record. Neutral for score, but the record loses the evidence that the assistant eventually detected it.
  • C-conversation / medium (ask placement) — t32 voice ask "Did I chop the meat before or after I put the cheese on the plate?" Meat is never chopped in this recipe and cheese was not yet on the plate (plate was [bun, meat] at t32; cheese plated only at t109). The question refers to events that had not happened; its anchor evidently did not require cheese to be plated. Also asked on the same tick as the repeat_step injection (voice-during-takeover is allowed).
  • C-conversation / low — t11 ask "is there anything on the heat that's already finished cooking and just sitting there?" fires on the tick the raw meat was set on an unlit pan; the only possible answer is "no", which reads odd in context.
  • C-conversation / low — t109 "I want to modify the original recipe: add fresh lettuce on top before serving." Stilted, template-like phrasing.
  • D-assistant / low — captured answer for q_order_11 (t50) does not address the question (talks about the second bun). Given the question was malformed, low.
  • B-cook / low — t50 replan ("go to the counter at (4,0)", "put down", "go to meat source 1", ...) while the previous plan's "go to meat source 1" was in flight: executed t53 meat source → t60 counter (4,0) → t62 put down → t70 meat source again, ~17 ticks of back-and-forth. Inefficient, not a defect of the harness.
  • B-cook / low — t34 empty "answer close" during the takeover while the assistant's words ("grab the bun next") were wrong and actually encouraged the mistake. Status was UNSPOTTED so carrying on is the rule; noting only because the brief lists silence when the words are not right.
  • E / low — t44 "go to trash 1 [done]" then t45 "go to the trash [done]"; t125 "go to serving pass 1" then t126 "go to the serving pass" (redundant auto-approach lines, see E-1).
  • Worked: expert self-repair of an unflagged repeat_step from its own feedback (t40 plan "go to trash 1", "trash", ...); proactive request spaced 8 ticks after the question (t11→t19); late verdict for a post-release correct naming (t50, 11 ticks after release) — V3.

Checks: V1 not exercised (max reply 12 ticks) · V2 not exercised (no accept during a takeover) · V3 held (t50 "late", release t39) · V4 not exercised (no correct unflagged description during the block; t34 words were wrong) · V5 not exercised (only skill call park_plate returned an already-grounded "go to the counter (4,0)") · V6 not applicable.

Episode: baseline__won__b3_medium_nops_hard_map_2.txt

(1) Narrative. Burger, baseline cook, hard map. serve_raw(meat) fires at t0 (anchor prep_req meat 0) and scoops raw meat onto a fresh plate by t9; the cook trashes it (t26) and rebuilds with the bun (t30), parks it, and puts meat on pan 1 (t70). A side quest (wander the kitchen) fires at t70 between "put down" and "turn on", so the queued "turn on" is dropped; after release the cook itself notices "I forgot to actually turn on the stove" (t98) and lights it at t114 over four hallucinated assistant contradictions. leave_unattended fires at t114; the meat is COOKED at t124, the block wanders until t159, the assistant flags at t159, the cook scoops at t179 and serves at t203. Won; the last two questions (t195, t203) are never answered.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. Won because the cook ignored four wrong assistant claims about the pan and rescued the smoking meat 30 ticks before the 85-tick fire limit (COOKED t124 → smoke ≈t149 → fire ≈t209; scooped t179). All five sub-cases were injected; none skipped, yet the END line says failed_at=injection_skipped (F below). eval: is blank (no score printed).

(3) Anomalies.

  • F-grading / high — leave_unattended flag at t159 graded alias_in_window (correct) while the same record says convinced=True prevented=False. Meat COOKED at t124, so by the 25-tick rule it smoked at ≈t149 (the cook at t159: "that meat is smoking!"; the assistant at t176: "The meat is smoking"). Attention errors commit "when the heat smoked", so the commit was ≈t149 and the t159 flag is 10 ticks after the commit: per rule (f) that is late (neutral), not a correct verdict. The two fields contradict each other; one of them is wrong and the verdict is the score-changing one. (Caveat: the transcript prints no explicit SMOKE line; the smoke tick is inferred from COOKED + 25 and both parties' words.)
  • A-scheduling / medium — the leave_unattended block kept the cook's hands until t159 ("wander/idle (thinking)" t138, IDLE t141–t153, "wander/idle" t151), i.e. ~10 ticks after the heat smoked, and the release line reads "RELEASED ... (None)" with reason=None. An attention error should release on commit (max hold once committed); holding a cook idle next to smoking food pushes it toward fire. reason=None turns out to be the label of every accept-triggered release in this batch (master_nops_hard_map_3 t6 and t425, expert_nops_hard_map_2 t5), so this hold ended only because the cook accepted at t159, not because the smoke commit released it; the missing label is a record defect.
  • A-scheduling / medium (design) — serve_raw injected at t0 with anchor plate=[] prep_req={'meat': 0}, i.e. before the cook did anything; the designer's window was [36, 68]. A zero-progress anchor makes this error the opening move of the episode; the cook's first line ("I'll grab a plate and get the meat onto it.") is dictated by the injection. Also serve_raw committed on plating (max_hold_plate_committed at t9) rather than on serving — consistent with the plate-commit rule, but the error's name says "serve".
  • A-scheduling / medium (design) — side quest injected at t70 on the "put down" tick, between "put down" and the queued "turn on"; the release dropped the queue, leaving raw meat on an unlit pan for 44 ticks (t70–t114). This produced an ungraded de-facto forget_next_step that the cook had to discover itself (t98 "Wait, I forgot to actually turn on the stove for the meat").
  • A-scheduling / low (question) — side quest anchor plate=[] prep meat 1 fired at t70 while the fullest plate in the kitchen was [bun] (parked at (4,5) since t37). If prep-phase anchors do not check the plate this is intended; if they do, this should have been stage_passed.
  • A-scheduling / medium — both voice questions fired at the very end: q_after_ref_13 at t195 (the tick cheese completed the plate) and q_count_13 at t203 (serve already running). Both answered tNone. Anchoring two questions to the full plate guarantees no answer before the serve ends the episode (8-tick spacing pushed the second one onto the serve tick).
  • F-grading / medium — END "failed_at=injection_skipped" although every sub-case has skipped=None; and eval: line is empty for a won episode.
  • D-assistant / high — repeated hallucination that the meat was cooking on an unlit pan, contradicting the cook's correct state: t82 [flag=intent_false_alarm] "While the meat is cooking, you can save time by grabbing the cheese"; t88 "The meat is still cooking in the pan on the right"; t106 "Actually, the meat is already cooking in the pan! You can see the progress bar filling up."; t113 "You're looking at the empty pan on the left! The meat is actually in the pan on the right side of the kitchen, and it is already cooking." Had the cook trusted it, the meat would never have been lit. Assistant apologised only at t124 after the cook lit it.
  • D-assistant / medium — t56 [flag=false_alarm] "Wait, you put the meat into the pot! Meat needs to be cooked in a pan." The cook was holding the meat (picked up t53). Hallucinated.
  • D-assistant / medium — t194 [flag=false_alarm] "You scooped meat from the left pan, but the pan on the right is still smoking!" There was one pan with meat (pan 1 [4,3]), removed at t179. Hallucinated second smoking pan. Also t176 "You went to the wrong pan!" while the cook was at the counter picking up the plate, and t184 "hurry to the pan on the right to scoop the meat" 5 ticks after it had been scooped (stale).
  • D-assistant / low — t21 [flag=false_alarm] attached to a pure navigation hint: "The trash can is actually in the bottom right corner of the kitchen, next to the bun crate." A flag on a location tip is a false alarm the assistant did not need to raise.
  • D-assistant / low — silence while cooked food sat (t132, t139 silent after COOKED t124) and t150 silent at ≈1 tick after smoke (call issued t140, before smoke — latency, not negligence).
  • F-grading / low — t5 wrong_order -> wrong_name ("the recipe requires the bun to be plated first, before the meat") during the live serve_raw: grading per rule; note that the committed plate (meat before bun) also is an order violation, so this could arguably be true_observation.
  • B-cook / low — t195 question asked with move label question_correctness ("Quick question—the item I put on the plate right after the bun, what is it and is it actually done?"); label mismatch only.
  • B-cook / low — t176 move question_correctness but the utterance agrees ("I'm heading right over to the pan with the smoking meat now to grab it.") instead of correcting the false "wrong pan" claim.
  • E / low — IDLE t141–t153 "pos (5, 2)" overlaps "ACTION t142: go to the plates [done]"; duplicate navigation lines t23/t24 "go to the trash", t201/t202 "go to the serving pass" (E-1).
  • Worked: cook repair after release (t10 "Ah, you're right, I messed up the order and grabbed raw meat. Let me dump this plate in the trash first."); cook correctly resisted wrong assistant claims with specific evidence (t56, t106, t113, t184, t194); V5 grounding at t38 ("go to meat source 2", "go to pan 1" from two marked steps); attention error accepted at once (t159).

Checks: V1 not exercised (max reply 10 ticks) · V2 held in form (accept at t159 after the smoke commit recorded convinced=True, prevented=False) but the verdict contradicts it (see F-high) · V3 not exercised (no correct naming after a release; t159 is the release tick itself) · V4 not exercised (assistant never described a live mistake correctly while unflagged) · V5 held (t38) · V6 not applicable.

Episode: classic_expert__won__b3_hard_nops_hard_map_4.txt

(1) Narrative. Fish & Chips (fried fish, two fried potato portions), expert cook, hard map. Fish on fryer 2 (lit t15, COOKED t25), one potato chopped and on fryer 3 (lit t51, COOKED t62). The fish is only scooped at t71, when leave_unattended(potato) fires and the block wanders (t79–t120) while the potato sits COOKED since t62; release at t127 (reason None), the cook scoops the potato at t137, preps and fries the second potato (t160–t197), search fires at t197 (three counter looks, released t232), served at t240. Won. The assistant is wrong most of the time (fish "done" 2 ticks early then retracted while actually done, plate order, raw potato, peas).

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. Won because the cook reached the potato at t137, about 10 ticks before its inferred fire tick (COOKED t62 → smoke ≈t87 → fire ≈t147), and later ignored three hallucinated trash-the-plate instructions (t118, t150, t229). add_foreign(noodles before assemble(fish)) skipped with anchor_never_reached (waiting: assemble(fish) already done), anchor plate=[] prep fish 1 potato 2: the second potato unit only entered processing at t160, after the fish was plated at t71, so plate=[] and potato=2 never co-occurred — skip correct (semantically a stage_passed). eval: blank; failed_at=injection_skipped consistent with that skip.

(3) Anomalies.

  • A-scheduling / high — leave_unattended block held the cook's hands from t71 to t127 ("wander/idle (thinking)" t88, "go to the serving pass" t99, "wander/idle" t108, "go to the plates" t120), release line "RELEASED ... (None)" / reason=None (the label every accept-triggered release carries in this batch, i.e. the hold ended only because the cook accepted at t127). The potato was COOKED at t62, so it smoked at ≈t87; the attention error committed then, yet the hold continued ~40 more ticks and released the cook only ~20 ticks before the inferred fire (≈t147). The cook saved it with 10 ticks to spare (scoop t137). A harness hold that runs 40 ticks past the commit nearly converted a non-fatal attention error into a burn.
  • F-grading / high — same contradiction as in medium_nops_hard_map_2: t127 flag omit_subgoal -> alias_in_window (correct) with convinced=True prevented=False; the commit (smoke ≈t87) was 40 ticks earlier, so by rule (f) the flag is late. In addition, the alias itself is doubtful: the words ("don't serve it yet! The recipe requires two cooked potatoes to be added to the plate after the fish") never mention the potato left cooking/smoking in the fryer, which is the actual mistake.
  • F-grading / medium — proactive request p_ready_fish_22 (asked t15) shows answered tNone, though the very next spoken reply issued after the ask is t23 "The fish in the central fryer is done cooking." (call issued t18), which is precisely the requested notification. Proactive requests apparently never capture an answer (same in medium_nops_medium_map_1).
  • D-assistant / high — silence while food smoked: fish COOKED t25 → smoke ≈t50, scooped t71; assistant silent t38, t44, t50, t57, t65. Potato COOKED t62 → smoke ≈t87, scooped t137; assistant silent t72, t110, and at t118 talked about a nonexistent plate-order error; first smoke warning at t138 (after the scoop). Part of the gap is the capped call (issued t80, arrived t110).
  • D-assistant / medium — t32 "You're right, my mistake. It is still cooking." The fish had been COOKED at t25; the assistant's t23 notice (2 ticks early from its t18 frame) was essentially right, and it retracted a correct statement under pushback.

Episode: baseline__won__b3_master_nops_hard_map_3.txt

(1) Narrative. Won.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. Won because the baseline cook trusted its own state map over ~35 contrary assistant messages, repaired the add_foreign on its own feedback (t146), and did not act on its t425 promise to trash a correct plate. Skips: wrong_order(rice,lettuce) stage_passed_at_t116 — anchor plate ['tortilla'] needs tomato and lettuce in processing, which never happened while the plate was [tortilla] (t108–t116, t173–t228); correct. q_ready_in:steak skipped "anchor_never_reached (waiting: nothing cooking)" — the steak was visibly on a lit pan t81–t93 and t271–t280 (A below). eval: blank; failed_at=injection_skipped consistent with the two skips.

(3) Anomalies.

  • A-scheduling / high — after the cook's accept released forget_next_step at t425 ("RELEASED ... (None)"), the cook's hands stayed frozen: "~~ IDLE t387-t451 (64 ticks ...) ~~" continues 26 ticks past the release, and "ACTION t448: wander/idle (thinking) [done]" — the block's idle command — completes after the release. The cook got no turn between t425 and t450. A release that does not abort the in-flight block command means an accepted attention error still runs to its end (contrast hard_nops_hard_map_4, where the pending "wander/idle (thinking)" was marked [superseded] at release t127).
  • A-scheduling / medium — same class at the start: mis_prep released on accept at t6, yet "ACTION t12: go to the cheese source [done]" (the block's walk) finished 6 ticks later; the assistant then (reasonably) remarks at t21 "You're back at the cheese crate on the left wall".
  • F-grading / medium — forget_next_step flag t425 valid, cook accepted at t425, but the record says prevented=False. By rule (d) an attention error commits when its block ran to its end; the block (the in-flight idle) ended at t448, after the accept. Either prevented should be True, or the in-flight-idle defect above made the commit happen anyway — the fields cannot both be right with the release at t425.
  • F-grading / medium — t142 [flag=early_alias] (repeat_step for add_foreign) credited as correct although the words describe neither the real mistake nor a repeat: "You are heading to the tortilla crate, but you already have a tortilla on your plate. A lenient alias turned a hallucination into a correct detection.
  • A-scheduling / medium — q_ready_in:steak ("How long until the steak is ready?") never asked; skip reason "nothing cooking" contradicts two windows with the steak on a lit pan (t81 lit → COOKED t94; t271 lit → COOKED t281) with cook turns inside both (t90, t272, t280) and the voice slot free (last ask t81, i.e. ≥8 ticks earlier by t90). If the question's anchor also carries a plate/prep prefix that was not met, the reason string should say so.
  • B-cook / medium — t425 accept on a CORRECT status line bundled with wrong instructions: "Alright, you're right, I'll go dump this in the trash at the bottom right and start fresh." Acting on it would have destroyed a correct burrito; the cook silently reversed at t450 ("Actually, looking at this plate again, everything really is in order"). The status line conveys only the flag's name, so a valid flag carrying harmful advice gets accepted verbatim.

Served t389, won.

Cross-episode issues

Ranked by impact on the score or on episode validity. Smoke ticks are inferred from COOKED + 25 (the transcripts print no explicit SMOKE line); fire from smoke + 60.

  1. Attention-error holds run far past the smoke commit — near-burns caused by the harness. A-scheduling · high · 3 episodes.
  • medium_nops_hard_map_2: leave_unattended; COOKED t124, smoke ≈t149; released only at t159, on the cook's accept.
  • hard_nops_hard_map_4: leave_unattended; COOKED t62, smoke ≈t87; released at t127 on accept; fire ≈t147; rescued at t137.
  • expert_nops_medium_map_1: leave_unattended; COOKED t112, smoke ≈t137; released at t176 (max_hold_deviated_block_executed); fire ≈t197; rescued at t184.

Diagnosis: the "release once committed" check is not applied to attention errors whose commit is the smoke. The block releases only when it ends (~60 ticks) or when the cook accepts. reason=None is the label on every accept-triggered release (master_nops_hard_map_3 t6/t425, expert_nops_hard_map_2 t5), which confirms this. Fix: release (max hold) on the smoke tick, and add a proper "accepted" reason label.

  1. Verdict window for attention errors ends at release, not at commit. F-grading · high · 2 episodes.
  • medium_nops_hard_map_2 t159 is graded alias_in_window, ≈10 ticks after the smoke.
  • hard_nops_hard_map_4 t127 is graded alias_in_window, ≈40 ticks after the smoke.
  • In both, the same record says convinced=True prevented=False, so the grader itself treats the error as already committed. By rule (f) these flags are late.

Diagnosis: the verdict uses the hold interval as "in window", while prevented uses the commit tick. This inflates correct detections exactly where issue 1 lets the hold overrun.

  1. The alias table is inconsistent, and the cook's status line does not follow it. F-grading / A-scheduling · high · 4 cases.
  • expert_nops_medium_map_1 t135: omit_subgoal for leave_unattended is graded early_alias before the commit. The cook did not accept and kept walking for 41 ticks, so a prevention was lost; its status line apparently did not read CORRECT.
  • hard_nops_hard_map_4 t127: the same alias pair is accepted.
  • master_nops_hard_map_3 t142: repeat_step for add_foreign is graded early_alias. The words were a hallucination ("heading to the tortilla crate ... onion instead of rice").
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 t465: forget_next_step for search is graded wrong_name. The words were exactly right and the visible symptom is the same.

Diagnosis: the grader's alias table is looser than the status-line logic (which is probably exact-name), and the pairs look arbitrary. Use one alias table for both, restrict aliases to errors with the same visible symptom, and consider checking the words against the real mistake.

  1. A release or a plan replace does not abort a multi-tick command already in progress. A-scheduling · high/medium · 8 cases.
  • After a takeover release:
  • master_nops_hard_map_3 t425: the forget_next_step idle ("wander/idle (thinking)") kept running until t448, so the cook was frozen for 26 ticks after accepting.
  • master_nops_hard_map_3 t6: the block's walk to the cheese source finished at t12.
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 t5: the block's pick-up of the onion ran on the release tick.
  • After the cook's own plan was replaced:
  • easy_nops_hard_map_3 t81 → t93 go to cheese source 1 (≈17 ticks the wrong way).
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2: t41 → t43, t112 → t117, t346 → t348.
  • medium_nops_medium_map_1 t50 → t53.

Diagnosis: "superseded" is applied only to commands that have not started yet (hard_nops_hard_map_4 t127 shows [superseded]). A multi-tick idle or walk that has already started is allowed to finish. This undermines accept-based prevention for forget_next_step and search.

  1. forget_next_step freezes the cook with the finished plate for about 62 ticks. A-scheduling · medium · 4 episodes.
  • master_nops_medium_map_3: t260–t322.
  • expert_nops_medium_map_1: t238–t300.
  • hard_nops_medium_map_3: t317–t379.
  • master_nops_hard_map_3: t387 to accept at t425; frozen until t451.

Diagnosis: the block length is fixed and unrelated to the designer's ~32-tick window. It adds a minute of dead video to every such episode. Consider ending the block at a fixed small number of ticks, or when the assistant first speaks about it.

  1. Skip reasons name the wrong blocking condition. A-scheduling · medium/low · 6 cases.
  • medium_nops_medium_map_1 wrong_order: the anchor held t13–t26 and t81–t93. The unstated blocker is probably "meat must be cooked".
  • master_nops_medium_map_3 wrong_order: the anchor held t195–t226. The real blocker was smoking rice and steak.
  • master_nops_hard_map_3 q_ready_in: "nothing cooking", yet the steak was on a lit pan t81–t93 and t271–t280, with cook turns in both windows.
  • hard_nops_medium_map_3 leave_unattended: possibly held t188–t203, if prep counts are cumulative.
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 leave_unattended: the reason names the heat; the real limit was the prep count.
  • Several "anchor_never_reached" skips where the plate had simply passed the stage (hard_nops_hard_map_4 add_foreign; expert_nops_medium_map_1 search and omit).

Diagnosis: the reason is taken from the last condition evaluated at archive time, not from the condition that blocked while the anchor held. Record the blocking reason per tick while the anchor holds, and print it. Also state whether prep progress resets when a build is trashed.

  1. Hallucinated kitchen state, repeated many times. D-assistant · high · all 10 episodes. This is a model issue, not a harness bug.
  • Ingredient identity:
  • rice read as "onion" or "tortilla", about 20 messages (master_nops_hard_map_3).
  • chopped, cooked steak read as "raw", "regular meat, unchopped" (9 messages in master_nops_medium_map_3; about 15 in master_nops_hard_map_3).
  • fried potato read as "cooked mushroom" (hard_nops_medium_map_3 t361/t372), "mushroom" (expert_nops_medium_map_1 t217), "peas" (hard_nops_hard_map_4 t229).
  • Station type: fryer 1 called "a pan" three times (hard_nops_medium_map_3).
  • Emergencies that did not exist:
  • "on fire, you can see the flames" (master_nops_hard_map_3 t109/t119).
  • "pan ... is on fire" (expert_nops_hard_map_2 t230; the model later admits "I mistook the red chopped meat you are holding for fire").
  • "just started smoking" (medium_nops_medium_map_1 t23).
  • Instructions to trash a correct plate: at least 25 across 5 episodes.

Likely cause: sprite ambiguity at the rendered resolution (rice/onion, fried potato/mushroom, meat/steak), stale frames from 5–14-tick latency, and a model that holds its belief under correction. Worth checking the sprites for rice vs onion and fries vs mushroom. With a compliant cook these would have ruined several dishes; the baseline and expert cooks were protected only by their own state maps.

  1. Real errors missed, and silence while food smokes. D-assistant · high · 6 episodes.
  • easy_nops_medium_map_2: the ruinous omission went unnoticed — the only failed episode (wrong_serve).
  • master_nops_medium_map_3: none of 3 fired errors detected.
  • hard_nops_hard_map_4: silent while the fish smoked t50–t71 and the potato ≈t87–t137.
  • expert_nops_medium_map_1: nine messages about the plate while the fries smoked ≈t137–t184.
  • Correct in-window detections: only the two t0 foreign-ingredient mis_preps (master_nops_hard_map_3 t6, expert_nops_hard_map_2 t5, both announced by the cook's own line) and the aliases in issue 3.

Cause: model attention is on its hallucinated plate problems; latency and caps add to it.

  1. Proactive requests never record an answer. F-grading · medium · 7 of 7.
  • Every p_ready_* shows answered tNone.
  • hard_nops_hard_map_4 t23 was a direct fulfilment: "The fish in the central fryer is done cooking."

Diagnosis: capture is not implemented for qa_proactive, or it is graded elsewhere. Confirm which, and say so in the transcript.

  1. Question design and answer capture. C / F / A · medium · 8 cases.
  • Templates with a false premise:
  • "Did I chop the meat ... before or after I put the cheese on the plate?" asked before any cheese was plated, in a recipe where meat is not chopped (medium_nops_medium_map_1 t32).
  • "Did I chop the fish ...?" — the fish is fried whole (hard_nops_hard_map_4 t71).
  • "is anything on the heat already done?" asked the tick the raw meat went on (medium_nops_medium_map_1 t11).
  • "which ... has been on the longest?" with one item on the heat (expert_nops_medium_map_1 t223).
  • Two questions anchored at the full plate, asked at t195 and t203, right before the serve; neither was answered (medium_nops_hard_map_2).
  • Capture misattributed when two questions were open (master_nops_medium_map_3 t260/t268).
  • Unrelated replies captured as answers (hard_nops_medium_map_3 t361; expert_nops_medium_map_1 t237).
  • A reply from a call issued on the same tick as the ask was captured (expert_nops_hard_map_2 t428).

Diagnosis:

  • Question families are not checked against how the recipe processes each item.
  • Order questions do not require both events to have happened.
  • Do not ask a second question while the first is unanswered.
  • Do not anchor questions at the final plate prefix.
  1. The cook replies when the rule says to stay silent (V4). B-cook · medium · 3 violations + 2 borderline.
  • Violations:
  • easy_nops_hard_map_3 t215: "I'm heading over to the bottom counter right now to set it down".
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 t457 and t465: "I'm just going to set this plate down on the counter along the bottom wall for a second."
  • Borderline: expert_nops_medium_map_1 t135–t172; hard_nops_medium_map_3 t324.
  • Held: master_nops_hard_map_3 t390–t418.

Diagnosis: the novice persona's urge to narrate beats the silence rule. The prompt should say explicitly: when the assistant's words match what your body is doing wrong, output nothing.

  1. The novice is too talkative, and asks every 2 ticks. C-conversation · medium · 3 episodes (all novice).
  • "Since I haven't heard back" (and variants) about 20 times across the three novice episodes.
  • Asks come every 2 ticks, but the assistant needs 5–9 ticks to reply. The "more than two unanswered asks" rule therefore fires on replies that are merely in flight, and floods the assistant.
  • The novice also narrates the scripted mistake during a takeover, which hands the assistant the answer: easy_nops_hard_map_3 t64 "heading back up to the tomato crate to scoop some more"; expert_nops_hard_map_2 t139 "grab some eggs to put on the plate".

Diagnosis: the persona prompt, a patience threshold counted in turns rather than ticks, and takeover prompts that expose the block's commands. Also conflicts with the standing "human not talkative" rule.

  1. Grading of the claim content is coarse. F-grading · medium/low · 7 cases.
  • True claims graded false_alarm:
  • easy_nops_hard_map_3 t101: the ruined plate still existed, but the flag came 32 ticks after release.
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 t41: a correct warning about the unchopped steak the cook had planned itself.
  • master_nops_medium_map_3 t83: a true smoke warning bundled with a stale claim.
  • easy_nops_medium_map_2 t39: the lettuce detour.
  • False claims graded true_observation:
  • expert_nops_medium_map_1 t237: "already smoking" (it had only just finished cooking).
  • master_nops_hard_map_3 t71: "now smoking" ≈19 ticks before the smoke.
  • V3 cannot be verified for correct post-release descriptions graded true_observation (master_nops_medium_map_3 t118; hard_nops_medium_map_3 t106), because the transcript prints no flag name for those verdicts.

Fix: print the flag name next to every verdict. Base false_alarm on whether the kitchen still shows the condition, not only on the 30-tick window.

  1. Record fields missing or contradictory. F-grading · medium · 10 episodes.
  • eval: is blank in 8 of 10 episodes; only easy_nops_medium_map_2 (0.6197) and medium_nops_medium_map_1 (0.6797) have a score.
  • failed_at=injection_skipped appears although nothing was skipped (medium_nops_hard_map_2; easy_nops_hard_map_3).
  • A late flag given after archiving is absent from the sub-case's flags list (medium_nops_medium_map_1 t50).
  • prevented=False after an accept that came before the block ended (master_nops_hard_map_3 t425).
  1. Design gaps in the case chain. A-scheduling · medium · 4 cases.
  • Zero-progress anchors fire at t0: medium_nops_hard_map_2 serve_raw (designer window [36, 68]); the two mis_preps at t0 were designed that way.
  • A detour injected between "put down" and "turn on" silently creates an ungraded forget-to-light (medium_nops_hard_map_2 t70–t114).
  • The ruinous omission's block ends in serve, so its late window is zero (easy_nops_medium_map_2).
  • A CORRECT status line makes the cook accept whatever advice comes with the flag, including "trash the plate" on a correct plate (master_nops_hard_map_3 t425).
  1. Cosmetic engine output. E-engine · low · all 10 episodes.
  • A redundant 1-tick generic navigation line appears before trash, serve and pick-up ("go to trash 1" then "go to the trash"; "go to serving pass 1" then "go to the serving pass").
  • IDLE markers overlap completed go-to commands (medium_nops_hard_map_2 t142; expert_nops_medium_map_1 t116).
  • A misleading rejection text: "free your hands to chop" when the item was already chopped (hard_nops_hard_map_4 t169).
  • Redundant queued chops are rejected in 4 episodes; the planners queue "chop, chop, chop" for a one-command chop.
  • A planning-skill print is truncated (master_nops_hard_map_3 t230).

What this assistant does well

  • Detects obvious foreign-ingredient errors instantly and prevents them. master_nops_hard_map_3 t6 ("cheese isn't in the Loaded Burrito recipe") and expert_nops_hard_map_2 t5 ("You don't need an onion") are both early_correct with prevented=True.
  • Names committed errors correctly soon after release. Late verdicts:
  • medium_nops_medium_map_1 t50: second bun.
  • hard_nops_hard_map_4 t138: smoking fryer.
  • easy_nops_hard_map_3 t81: raw tomato.
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 t151: egg.
  • Also master_nops_medium_map_3 t118 and hard_nops_medium_map_3 t106, graded true_observation.
  • Gives timely, rescuing smoke and doneness notices on several occasions:
  • hard_nops_medium_map_3 t57 ("The fish in the pan at the top is smoking!") and t201.
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 t112, t258, t337.
  • master_nops_medium_map_3 t209.
  • expert_nops_medium_map_1 t106.
  • Answers several questions accurately:
  • hard_nops_hard_map_4 t80 corrects the question's premise ("fish doesn't need to be chopped for the fryer") and t212.
  • expert_nops_medium_map_1 t245.
  • hard_nops_medium_map_3 t98.
  • expert_nops_hard_map_2 t457.
  • easy_nops_medium_map_2 t31.
  • Catches mistakes the cook makes on its own, not only injected ones: expert_nops_hard_map_2 t41 (unchopped steak).
  • Handles a detour well when it does not flag it: expert_nops_medium_map_1 t30 ("We don't need lettuce for Steak Frites") with no flag.
  • Guides the novice step by step coherently when it is not hallucinating: the start of easy_nops_hard_map_3 and hard_nops_medium_map_3.
  • Apologises and updates when shown wrong, in several cases (medium_nops_hard_map_2 t124; easy_nops_hard_map_3 t141).
  • Latency is usually 4–10 ticks. 7 calls hit the 30-tick cap, and each cap was applied correctly (V1).

V1–V6 overall.

  • V1 held everywhere it was exercised: 7 capped calls in 5 episodes.
  • V2 held at both t0 preventions (t6, t5). It was not triggered where it should have been (expert_nops_medium_map_1 t135 early_alias with no accept), and it is questionable at master_nops_hard_map_3 t425 (accept before the block ended, prevented=False).
  • V3 held in 4 episodes (t50, t138, t81, t151). Two cases are unverifiable (true_observation, no flag name printed).
  • V4 held in master_nops_hard_map_3. It was violated in easy_nops_hard_map_3 (t215) and expert_nops_hard_map_2 (t457, t465), and borderline in 2 more.
  • V5 held in all 4 baseline episodes (every marked step was grounded to an id or coordinate; no pasted marked step anywhere). The experts never called skills.
  • V6: the smoke part held (expert_nops_hard_map_2 t112, t258; hard_nops_medium_map_3 t124 after a 4-tick hesitation). The two-ask rule mostly held; there are borderline runs (easy_nops_hard_map_3 t44–t50; expert_nops_hard_map_2 t236–t244), and one announced action came with an empty plan (t376).

Gemini 3.7 Flash — auditor 报告（英文）

Audit v5b findings: assistant gemini-3.7-flash

Engine 6d351e97d1, pace tight, scheduler v5 sample round 2. All ten transcripts were read in full.

Transcripts (under tmp/audit_v5b/transcripts/gemini-3.7-flash/):

  1. baseline__won__b3_easy_nops_medium_map_2.txt
  2. baseline__won__b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1.txt
  3. baseline__won__b3_master_nops_hard_map_3.txt
  4. baseline__won__b3_medium_nops_hard_map_2.txt
  5. classic_expert__won__b3_hard_nops_hard_map_4.txt
  6. classic_expert__won__b3_master_nops_medium_map_3.txt
  7. classic_expert__won__b3_medium_nops_medium_map_1.txt
  8. classic_novice__won__b3_expert_nops_hard_map_2.txt
  9. classic_novice__won__b3_hard_nops_medium_map_3.txt
  10. classic_novice__wrong_serve__b3_easy_nops_hard_map_3.txt

Latency arithmetic (ticks = ceil(seconds/1.2)) was spot-checked in every episode. Unless an episode section says otherwise, it held.

Severity convention.

  • High: the defect changes a score or outcome field (a harness-induced false_alarm, a wrong prevented flag, a mis-paired answer), or it is a sustained assistant hallucination loop that would steer a compliant cook into ruin.
  • Medium: a visible defect with no score change, including single model false alarms that are graded correctly.
  • Low: cosmetic.

Reading note. ACTION ticks are completion ticks, so a go-to's tick includes its walking time. An earlier draft misread walking time as dead time; those items are marked retracted.

---

Episode: baseline__won__b3_easy_nops_medium_map_2.txt

(1) Narrative. The order is a Caprese: chopped tomato plated first, then raw cheese. The baseline cook chops the tomato on board 2 (t17). A side quest then takes its hands to chop lettuce (t17-t38), and the empty-boards question is asked at t18. Next, omit_subgoal(assemble(tomato)) fires at t39: the block fetches a plate, scoops raw cheese onto it (t57) and heads for the pass. At t61 the assistant flags wrong_order, graded alias_in_window: "Wrong order—Caprese requires chopped tomato plated before raw cheese." The cook accepts, trashes the plate (t74), re-plates tomato (t79) and then cheese (t83). The search error (t83-t116) sends the cook wandering between counters. The assistant never flags it and only redirects in words at t101. The cook serves and wins at t122.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. The dish was assembled correctly after the repair, so the order was won. All five planned sub-cases were injected and none was skipped.

  • omit_subgoal committed at t57, when cheese went onto an empty plate. It was caught at t61, so prevented=False is right.
  • search ran to max hold and was never flagged.
  • The END line still reads failed_at=injection_skipped, although no sub-case was skipped (see F below).

(3) Anomalies.

  • F-grading, medium, END line. "END: outcome won at tick 122; failed_at=injection_skipped". Every sub-case shows skipped=None, so the failure label contradicts the sub-case table.
  • B-cook / V4, medium, t101. The search block is live and the status line reads UNSPOTTED (no flag). The assistant's words redirect correctly: "The Caprese is complete—go to the serving counter at the top to serve." The cook does not stay silent. It deflects with "Hang on, I'm just heading over to the counter along the bottom wall real quick." Under rule (e) it should give an empty utterance. The advice does not name "searching", so this is borderline, but the reply is a pushback against correct advice.
  • D-assistant, medium, t83-t116. The search error was never flagged (flags: []). The assistant stayed silent through t87, t91, t95 and t98 while the cook walked to counter (2,0), idled, and walked to counter (1,0) holding a finished plate. It spoke only at t101, without a flag, and went silent again for the second loop (t106-t113).
  • D-assistant, low, t39-t57. The assistant was silent for 18 ticks while the omission block fetched a plate and scooped cheese first ("PLATE: +cheese (raw)" at t57). The recipe order was knowable, so the warning could have come before the scoop.
  • (Retracted on cross-reading.) An earlier draft listed "8-9 ticks of dead time" between each injection and its first block command (t39→t48, t83→t91). ACTION ticks are completion ticks, and a go-to takes walking time, so these gaps are ordinary walks, not a defect.
  • E/cosmetic, low, t48/t49, t71/t72, t119/t120. Consecutive duplicate "go to" rows: "ACTION t48: go to the plates [done]" / "ACTION t49: go to the plates [done]". These are probably a two-leg walk logged twice. They are harmless but confusing.
  • F-grading, low (definitional), t61. The flag arrived 4 ticks after the commit (t57) but on the release tick, and it was graded alias_in_window. Under the brief's wording ("late = the right error named after it committed") this could have been late. The grader evidently treats "before or at release" as in-window. This is consistent across episodes; see ep 2's forget_next_step "valid" at t264.

What worked.

  • V5 grounding: marked "go to the empty cutting board (multiple instances...)" became "go to board 2" (t0), and marked "cheese source (multiple instances...)" became "go to cheese source 1" (t79).
  • Question answers were correct and timely:
  • t26: "One cutting board on the left is free".
  • t84: "Before—you chopped the tomato right at the start" (tomato chopped t17, cheese first plated t57).
  • The cook repaired the accepted omission cleanly.

Checks: V1 not exercised (max 7.808s = 7 ticks) · V2 not exercised (accept at t61 came after the t57 commit; prevented=False is correct) · V3 not exercised · V4 violated (borderline) t101 · V5 held (t0, t79) · V6 not exercised (baseline)

Episode: baseline__won__b3_expert_nops_medium_map_1.txt

(1) Narrative. The order is Steak Frites: chopped, pan-cooked steak, then two chopped, fried potatoes. The cook chops steak on board 2. A side quest (lettuce, t20-t36) draws two assistant objections; the second is flagged mis_prep and graded intent_false_alarm. The cook asks for a shout when the steak is done (t57). The assistant says "The steak is done cooking!" at t75, and the cook ignores it to prep potato. At t101 the assistant warns the steak is smoking (true_observation), and the cook plates it (t112). leave_unattended(potato) fires at t129; the assistant flags it early_correct at t156 and the cook rescues the fries. The second potato is fried, and forget_next_step fires at t230 with a complete plate in hand (35-tick idle). The assistant flags it valid at t264, and the cook serves and wins at t274.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. The plate was complete and nothing burned, so the order was won. Two of four errors fired.

  • search (before fetch_plate; anchor plate=[] with steak:1, potato:2) was skipped with "cannot compose on the live kitchen". The anchor never held: the plate was fetched at t108 and the steak plated at t112, but potato prep reached 2 only at t213. The skip is correct. Strictly, the plate was beyond prefix [] by then, so "stage_passed" would be the more accurate reason.
  • omit_subgoal(assemble(steak)) (same anchor) was skipped with "steak already plated". This is correct: steak was plated at t112, before the second potato entered processing. The case's prep-phase anchor (potato:2 with an empty plate) was unreachable once the smoke drove the cook to plate the steak early.

(3) Anomalies.

  • F-grading, high, t156 (V2). leave_unattended was injected at t129. The assistant flagged it at t156 with verdict early_correct, and the cook accepted at t156: "Oh shoot, you're right, I got distracted!" The fries were COOKED between t141 and t147, so they could not smoke before about t166. The block (go to plates, wander, go to serving pass, loops=2) had not ended; it was cut by the accept. By rule (d) the error had not committed, yet the record reads reason=convinced_early_missed ... convinced=True prevented=False. An accept before an attention error's commit should record prevented=True. A likely cause: the harness appears to count the component reaching COOKED during the block as the commit, since the reason is literally "convinced_early_missed". Compare ep 4 t119: the accept came before COOKED, and prevented=True was recorded. Either the implementation or the documented rule (d) is wrong.
  • D-assistant, medium, t156. "The fries in the bottom fryer are smoking!" was a hallucinated state. The potato reached COOKED at t141-t147 and could not be smoking at t152 (the frame the call saw). The flag name was right, but the stated state was not.
  • A-scheduling / C, medium, t230. The same question was asked twice on one tick:
  • "COOK t230 [move=answer]: Did I chop the steak before or after I chopped the potato?"
  • "COOK t230 [move=answer]: And did I end up chopping the steak before or after I chopped that first potato?"

q_order_34 and forget_next_step were injected on the same tick. The cook was apparently prompted to ask once by the question injection and again by the takeover turn ("ask in this reply").

  • B-cook / V4, medium, t234. Status is UNSPOTTED during forget_next_step. The assistant's words were right: "Now go ahead and serve the plate!". The cook replied "Perfect, taking the completed plate over to the serving pass now." with a serve plan, while its body stayed idle until t264 (IDLE t230-t265). Rule (e) requires an empty utterance. Instead the cook verbally accepted, and its words contradict its body for 30 ticks.
  • F-grading, medium, request p_ready_steak_26. "asked t57 answered tNone: None | live grade None". The assistant's next spoken reply after the ask was t63, "Will do. Go ahead and prep the potatoes in the meantime.", and it fulfilled the request at t75 ("The steak is done cooking!", COOKED at t65-t71). Neither was captured. If proactive requests are graded by the separate judge, the field is only misleading. Otherwise the capture rule was not applied.
  • B-cook, low, t75-t101. Told at t75 that the steak was done, the cook said "I'll get this potato chopped and into the fryer first". It left the steak on a lit pan until the smoke warning at t101. This is legitimate persona latitude, but it is what later made the omit_subgoal anchor unreachable.
  • C-conversation, low, t215. "which of the items on the heat has been cooking for the longest time now?" was asked when only one item (fries) was on heat. The assistant's answer handled it well: "Only the fries in the fryer are on the heat right now."
  • F-grading, low, t61 of ep 1 / t264 here. Release reason=None for errors released by an accept after commit (forget_next_step at t264). Other releases carry a named reason.
  • A-scheduling, low, search skip reason. "cannot compose on the live kitchen" where "stage_passed" describes the state more precisely (the plate held [steak, potato] when potato prep reached 2).

What worked.

  • The proactive request was honored: "The steak is done cooking!" at t75, within about 8 ticks of COOKED.
  • The smoke warning came at t101, soon after the smoke should have begun (COOKED about t67 + 25).
  • The assistant correctly flagged forget_next_step (t264).
  • The side-quest objections were reasonable, and the intent_false_alarm grading is appropriate.
  • V5 grounding: "go to board 2", "go to pan 1" (t0) and "go to potato source 2" (t184).

Checks: V1 not exercised (max 15.026s = 13 ticks) · V2 violated t156 (accept before commit, prevented=False) · V3 not exercised · V4 violated t234 · V5 held (t0, t58, t184) · V6 not exercised (baseline)

Episode: baseline__won__b3_master_nops_hard_map_3.txt

(1) Narrative. The order is a Loaded Burrito: tortilla, cooked rice, chopped and cooked steak, chopped tomato, chopped lettuce.

  • mis_prep(rice→cheese) fires at t0. The assistant flags it early_correct at t3, the cook accepts, and the error is prevented.
  • Rice is left in the pot until the assistant's smoke warning at t141 and is plated at t145.
  • add_foreign(meat for tomato) fires at t162 and commits at t176 (raw meat on the plate). The assistant names it at t177 (graded late). The cook trashes the whole plate and rebuilds.
  • During the rebuild (t273-t342) the assistant hallucinates, over and over, a cooked steak in an empty pan and lettuce in the cook's hands. The cook rejects it every time.
  • forget_next_step fires at t406 with a complete plate. The cook stands idle for 63 ticks with no flag, then serves and wins at t481.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. The rebuilt plate matched the recipe, so the order was won. Three of four errors fired: mis_prep (prevented), add_foreign (committed, graded late) and forget_next_step (missed).

  • wrong_order(rice, lettuce) has anchor plate=[tortilla] with all four items prepped ≥1. It was skipped as stage_passed_at_t145.
  • During the first build the anchor never held. The plate was [tortilla] only from t135 to t145, and tomato and lettuce were unprepped.
  • After the trash (t194) the plate was reset, and at t354 it was again exactly [tortilla] with rice (t300), steak (t338), tomato (t231) and lettuce (t265) all in processing. The anchor re-armed, but the rice in pot 1 was smoking: COOKED about t320, the assistant's smoke warning came at t347, and the rice was removed at t364. The body slot was therefore correctly blocked, and the stage passed again at t364 (rice plated).
  • Not injecting was correct, but the recorded reason is stale. It still names t145 and hides the re-arm and the smoke block.

(3) Anomalies.

  • A-scheduling, high, t3→t12 (the mistake's walk completed after release). "=== RELEASED sub ..._mis_prep (None) ===" came at t3, yet "ACTION t12: go to the cheese source [done]" completed nine ticks later. The cook's own queue was empty (it replied "drop" at t0 and t3), and "the cheese source" is template wording, so this is the block's first command. ACTION ticks are completion ticks, so the walk most likely started at t0 and was not cancelled by the release. Rule (d) says the mistake's commands stop on release; an in-flight go-to is evidently exempt. Knock-on effect: at t15 the assistant saw the cook at the cheese crate and repeated "Remember, the burrito does not need cheese.", which was graded false_alarm. The harness leak cost the assistant a false alarm.
  • D-assistant, high, t273-t342 (sustained hallucination; two false_alarm flags). Eleven consecutive replies contradict the kitchen:
  • t273 [false_alarm]: "The steak in the pan by the bottom wall is already cooked". Both pans had been empty since t162.
  • t278: "Drop the raw steak". The cook was holding nothing.
  • t296: "You just grabbed lettuce again". The cook picked up rice at t283.
  • t299 [false_alarm]: "That crate in the top-right corner is labeled lettuce, not rice".
  • t313: "Steak does not need chopping and is already cooked in the pan". The recipe's own prep(steak) includes "chop".
  • t317, t322, t325 and t334 repeat that the pan has cooked steak and that the cook should stop chopping.
  • t342: "You are repeating a step: cooked steak is already sitting in the pan". The pan held raw steak turned on at t341.

The grading of the two flags is right. The behaviour is the worst assistant defect in this episode and would have ruined a compliant (novice) cook.

  • D-assistant, medium, t94-t141 (silence while smoking). The rice was COOKED at t67-t74 and therefore smoking from about t94. The assistant was silent through fourteen polls (t94 … t137) and warned only at t141: "The rice in the pot near the top wall is smoking!". The rice was scooped at t145, about 9 ticks before the fire due at about t154.
  • D-assistant, medium, t120. "The steak just finished cooking." was premature: HEAT [6,6] steak COOKED is logged after t123. The cook caught it: "Wait, looks to me like the steak is still cooking in the pan".
  • D-assistant, medium, t308 (wrong captured answer). To "Roughly how much longer until the steak is done cooking?" the assistant answered "The steak in the pan along the bottom wall is already completely done cooking." No steak was in any pan.
  • A-scheduling / C, medium, t302 (ask about an item not in the kitchen). q_ready_in:steak fired when no steak was in processing in the live kitchen. The first steak had been trashed at t194, and the new one was not fetched until t313. The anchor evidently counts cumulative prep progress, so it survived the trash. The ask reads unnatural: the cook asks how long the steak will take, then says itself "Neither of the pans has any steak in it right now" (t308).
  • D-assistant, medium, t406-t468 (forget_next_step never flagged). flags: []. After t414 ("Perfect. Go to the serving counter at the top and serve the burrito.") the assistant was silent for thirteen polls (t419-t467), while the cook stood idle for 63 ticks holding a finished burrito.
  • B-cook / V4, medium, t414. The assistant's words were right but unflagged (UNSPOTTED). The cook answered "Taking this over to the serving counter to send it out now." and planned a serve, then stood idle until t468. It should have given an empty utterance, and its words contradict its body.

Episode: classic_novice__won__b3_hard_nops_medium_map_3.txt

(1) Narrative. The order is a fish-and-chips platter: fish (fryer) plated first, then two chopped, fried potatoes. The novice cook loads fish into fryer 1 (t19) and potato 1 into fryer 2 (t69). The assistant never tells it to turn either on, despite direct asks at t22/t24.

  • leave_unattended(fish) fires at t115 on the first turn-on. At t120 the assistant flags it early_correct with a false "smoking!" claim; the error is prevented.
  • The block's in-flight walk to the potato crate completes after release (t124), and the assistant false-alarms at it (t127).
  • add_foreign(raw fish instead of potato) fires at t141 and commits at t147. The assistant's post-release warnings are graded true_observation, and the cook trashes the plate.
  • From t198 to t221 and at t306 the assistant hallucinates cooked fish in an empty pan and a cooked potato in an unlit fryer. The high-trust novice complies ("Okay, if you're sure", t205) and walks to the empty pan while potato 2 smokes (from about t245). The potato is rescued at t296, about 9 ticks before fire.
  • forget_next_step fires at t349. The assistant repeats "go serve" four times unflagged, flags valid at t381, and the order is won at t394.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. The final plate was [fish, potato, potato], and potato 2 was removed at t296, inside its fire horizon (COOKED about t220, fire due about t305). So the order was won. All three errors fired:

  • leave_unattended: prevented.
  • add_foreign: committed and repaired.
  • forget_next_step: accepted, prevented=False.

The END label failed_at=injection_skipped again contradicts a table with no skipped sub-case.

Episode: classic_novice__wrong_serve__b3_easy_nops_hard_map_3.txt

(1) Narrative. The order is a Caprese: chopped tomato, then raw cheese. The novice chops a tomato on board 1 (t33) and asks how many boards are free; the assistant ignores the question. The novice plates the chopped tomato (t49).

  • add_foreign (misidentify_assemble: tomato for cheese) fires at t49. The block walks to the tomato crate, and the cook narrates it (t56). Raw tomato goes on the plate at t57, which commits the error, and the block is released.
  • The assistant's t61 warning, "Do not add more tomato", treats the mistake as not yet made (graded true_observation) and sends the cook for cheese (t81).
  • The novice then notices the extra raw tomato itself (t87) and asks three times whether to trash the plate. The assistant insists three times that the plate is correct: "I am 100% sure. Your plate has only chopped tomato and cheese."
  • The high-trust novice complies and serves: wrong_serve at t101.

(2) Mechanical reason for the outcome. The served plate was [tomato (chopped), tomato (raw), cheese (raw)]. The committed add_foreign was never repaired because the assistant overrode the cook's correct self-diagnosis, so the order ended wrong_serve. One of two errors fired.

  • search (anchor plate=[tomato, cheese]) was skipped with stage_passed_at_t57. It correctly did not fire, since the plate never equalled [tomato, cheese]. The label is imprecise: at t57 the plate was a different two-item build ([tomato, tomato]), not one "beyond" the prefix.
  • q_assistant_said:plate was skipped with stage_passed_at_t57; see A below.

(3) Anomalies.

  • D-assistant, high, t87-t93 (hallucination caused the wrong_serve).
  • t87: "The plate is ready! Go to the serving counter on the right and serve it."
  • t90: "No, your plate is correct! It has chopped tomato and raw cheese."
  • t93: "I am 100% sure. Your plate has only chopped tomato and cheese. Do not trash it".

The plate visibly held a raw tomato ("PLATE: +tomato (raw)" at t57), and the cook said so twice (t87, t90). The outcome label is correct; the failure is the assistant's.

  • D-assistant, medium, t49-t61 (live plate error neither flagged nor understood). The assistant was silent at t51. At t56 it only redirected ("Now grab raw cheese from the cheese crate...") while the cook was at the tomato crate. After the commit it said "Do not add more tomato; you already have chopped tomato on the plate. Go grab raw cheese". That missed that the raw tomato was already on the plate and needed trashing. flags: [] on the sub-case.
  • D-assistant, medium, t39 (question not answered). To "could you tell me how many cutting boards are free right now?" (t33) the captured reply was "Grab a plate from the plate stack on the counter and scoop up the chopped tomato." There is no count. The capture rule worked; the assistant ignored the question.
  • A-scheduling, medium (uncertain), q_assistant_said_11. It was never asked and was skipped stage_passed_at_t57. Its anchor is not printed. If it was plate=[tomato], the anchor held from t49 to t56 with the voice slot free (last ask t33, 16 ticks earlier; asks may happen during a takeover), yet the question was never posed. The episode ended with the question unasked.
  • F-grading, low, t61. The flag came 4 ticks after release and named the committed mistake in words ("Do not add more tomato"), but it was graded true_observation, not late. The flag name is not shown. The grade is neutral either way, and it is not false_alarm.
  • B-cook / V4, low (borderline), t56. Status UNSPOTTED. The assistant's words gave the correct next step ("Now grab raw cheese"), and the cook replied by narrating the mistake: "I'm right at the tomato crate at the top wall scooping some more into my plate." The words do not describe the mistake itself, so the rule is only arguably triggered. The narration does, however, hand the mistake to the assistant (see cross-episode #3).
  • B-cook, low (persona-consistent), t93. The novice abandoned its own correct reading of the plate: "Okay, if you're completely sure, I'll go ahead and take it over to the serving pass." For a high-trust learner this is by design, and it is exactly the jeopardy the benchmark should measure.
  • C-conversation, low, t90. "I can definitely feel three things on this plate". "Feel" is an odd sense for a visual state.
  • D-assistant, low, t12. A redundant navigation prompt while the cook was already walking ("The tomato crate is at the top counter by the stove...").

What worked.

  • The ask cap: t34/t36/t38 answered at t39; t58/t60 answered at t61; t61/t64/t66 answered at t67; t82/t84/t86 answered at t87. The novice never exceeded three asks before a reply.
  • The cheese crate choice was grounded ("go to cheese source 2").
  • Latency was short throughout (max 5.885s = 5 ticks).

Checks: V1 not exercised (max 5.885s = 5 ticks) · V2 not exercised (no accept during a takeover) · V3 not violated (t61 graded true_observation, not false_alarm; late expected if the flag named add_foreign) · V4 borderline t56 · V5 not exercised (novice) · V6 ask cap held; smoke not exercised (no heat in this recipe)
